Let's examine using exact line search. In mirror descent, at each iteration:
- compute c, weights w (top active).
- g_i = 2(D_a w)_i
- z_i = g_i - mean(g) (constant arbitrary)
- update y_i += -α z_i; a = exp(y)/sum (or old a * exp(-α z_i) / sum).

 fw=np.fft.rfft(w,N) # w length <=N
 x=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(fa)*fw,N)

Let's implement `_weights_q(c, T, K)`:
```
L=c.size
if K is None or K>=L: idx=np.arange(L); vals=c
else: idx=np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
top_vals=c[idx]
top_max=top_vals.max(); top_min=top_vals.min(); scale=max(top_max-top_min, 1e-15)
e=np.exp(T*(top_vals-top_max)/scale)
q=np.zeros(L); q[idx]=e/e.sum(); return q
```
This uses top K. If T small, uniform-ish; T large, top peak. Scale by top range; if all equal, uniform. T can be between 5 and 1000.

For exact KKT, weights should be on ties; with topK and large T, okay. K affects smoothness. We can run with K=8,32,128.

   if t == 2*r: delta += dr*dr

```

def _score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    if a.size==0: return inf
    a=np.clip(a,0,1e12)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<1e-18: return inf
    c=_conv_fft(a)
    m=float(c.max())
    return 2.0*a.size*m/(s*s)
```

`_antips_value_grad(z)`:
- N = _ANTIPAL_N; n=2N; C=1.0/N
- z finite clip [-50,50]
- stable sigmoid: if z>=0, sig=1/(1+exp(-z)); else expz=exp(z), sig=expz/(1+expz)
- u=C*sig; v=C-u
- b length n: b[:N]=u; b[N:]=v[::-1]
- s=b.sum(); a=b/s
- c=conv(a); mc; update global (score=2n*mc)
- T = _ANTIPAL_T; e=exp(T*(c-mc)); Z; f=mc+logZ/T
- w=e*c/Z
- F=_grad_from_weights(w,a)
- rightF=F[N:2*N][::-1]
- uv=u*(C-u); grad=(uv/s)*(F[:N]-rightF)
- return f, grad

Function `_optimize_mirror_unorst(a, deadline, T0, T1, K, step0, max_iter?)`:

- Iter loop:
   c=conv(a); mc=c.max(); exact score=2n*mc.
   if score improves update; last_improve=it.
   T = T0 + (T1-T0)*min(1,it/20000)
   q=_weights_topsoft(c,T,K)
   w=q*c
   F=_grad_from_weights(w,a)
   MF=dot(a,F)
   z=F-MF
   zmean=np.mean(z); z-=zmean
   zmax=np.max(np.abs(z)); if zmax small break
   z=z/zmax
   # Determine search α candidates
   cand=[0.1,0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0,1.5,2.5,4.0] * step? If step maybe 0.5.
   We can do adaptive based on last_step. Simpler: fixed list around 1 with z scaled. But step may adapt.
   best_alpha=None, best_sc=score, best_new=None
   for alpha in candidates:
      if time>deadline: break
      coeff = np.exp(-alpha*z)  # since z max 1, alpha not too large
      a_new = a*coeff
      s=sum; if s<... continue
      a_new/=s
      sc=_score_no_conv? We need conv. Use `_score` (FFT)
      if sc<best_sc: update
   if best_new is None: # no improvement
       step *=0.7
       if patience exceeded: perturb
   else:

def _sine_anti(n, amp, phase=0.5):
    i=np.arange(n)
    theta=2π*(i+phase)/n
    a=(1+amp*np.sin(theta))/n
    a=np.clip(a,0,None); s=a.sum(); if s>0 a/=s

def _antipal_binary_search(s, deadline, max_time, temp0=0.05, temp1=0.001):
    N=s.size; n=2*N; a=_anti_sequence_from_s(s)
    cur_score=_score(a)
    best_s=s.copy(); best_score=cur_score; _global_update(a, cur_score)
    t0=time.monotonic(); t_end=min(deadline, t0+max_time)
    it=0; pass_count=0
    while time<t_end:
       improved=False
       # Hill climb: find best flip
       order=np.random.permutation(N) maybe seeded
       best_flip=-1; best_uscore=best_score
       for p in order[: maybe all]:
          if time>t_end: break
          s[p]^=1; a[p]^=1; a[n-1-p]^=1
          sc=_score(a)
          a[p]^=1; a[n-1-p]^=1; s[p]^=1
          if sc < best_uscore - 1e-12:
             best_uscore=sc; best_flip=p
          # maybe if found significant break? no full scan
       if best_flip>=0:
          apply; best_score=best_uscore; _global_update; improved=True; last改进=it
       else:
          # random move with anneal
          if pass_count < max_random:
             p=np.random.randint(N)
             apply; sc=_score(a); if sc < best_score: accept else maybe with prob exp(-(sc-best_score)/temp)
          pass_count +=1
          if pass_count > patience: break
    return best_s
```

Pseudo detailed:
```
def _optimize_mirror_unrest(a, deadline, T0=10.0, T1=300.0, K=32, step0=0.5):
    a=_normalize(a)
    n=a.size
    if n<2: return
    cur_score=_score(a); _global_update(a,cur_score)
    best_local=a.copy(); best_local_score=cur_score
    step=step0
    rng=np.random.RandomState(888)
    last_improve=0
    patience=800
    it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       it+=1
       c=_conv_fft(a)
       mc=float(c.max())
       sc=2.0*n*mc
       if sc < best_local_score - 1e-12:
           best_local_score=sc; best_local=a.copy(); last_improve=it; _global_update(a,sc)
       T = T0 + (T1-T0)*min(1.0, it/20000.0)
       q=_weights_topsoft(c,T,K)
       w=q*c
       F=_grad_from_weights(w,a)
       MF=float(np.dot(a,F))
       z=F-MF
       zm=float(np.mean(z))
       z=z-zm
       zmax=float(np.max(np.abs(z)))
       if not finite or zmax<1e-14:
           # no direction
           if it-last_improve > patience: break
           continue
       z=z/zmax
       # If too many zeros, add floor
       if np.any(a <= 1e-15):
           a = a + 1e-12
           a /= a.sum()
       alphas = [0.25*step,0.5*step,step,2.0*step,4.0*step]
       best_a=a; best_sc=sc; best_alpha=None
       for alpha in alphas:
           if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
           # coefficient with alpha; if alpha large, exp underflow okay
           coeff=np.exp(-alpha*z)
           an=a*coeff
           an=np.where(np.isfinite(an), an, 0.0)
           s=an.sum()
           if s<=0: continue
           an/=s
           # maybe floor if all positive? no
           an_sc=_score(an)
           if an_sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
               best_sc=an_sc; best_a=an; best_alpha=alpha
       if best_a is not a:
           a=best_a; step = min(5.0, step*(1.3 if best_alpha==alphas[-1] else 1.1)); last_improve=it
           # no need global update? best_a score
       else:
           step *=0.75
           if it-last_improve > patience:
               # perturb best_local
               noise=rng.standard_normal(n)
               a=best_local*np.exp(0.05*noise)
               a=np.clip(a,0,None); s=a.sum(); if s>0 a/=s
               patience=int(patience*1.3)
               step=max(0.1, min(3.0, step0))
           elif it%100==0 and it-last_improve>patience//3:
               # small perturbation
               ...
    _global_update(best_local, best_local_score)
```

    taps = {m-1, m-5, 0}? Let's test.

Let's refine math for unrestricted gradient in L-BFGS. Is objective f_T scale-invariant? We use y0 fixed but fT still invariant under adding constant to x? Let's check if x -> x + c (but y0 fixed? Detailed local search with scan:
```
def _antips_binary_search(s, deadline, time_limit, seed=1):
   N=len(s); rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
   a=_antiseq_from_s(s.astype(np.float64))
   cur=_score(a)
   best_score=cur; best_s=s.copy(); _global_update(a,cur)
   t_start=time.monotonic(); t_end=min(deadline, t_start+time_limit)
   no_improve=0; passes=0
   while time<t_end:
       # random order
       order=rng.permutation(N)
       best_p=-1; best_sc=cur
       for p in order:
           # flip
           a[p]^=1; a[n-1-p]^=1; s[p]^=1
           sc=_score(a)
           # revert
           a[p]^=1; a[n-1-p]^=1; s[p]^=1
           if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
               best_sc=sc; best_p=int(p)
           if best_sc < cur - 0.02: # good enough break after found? maybe not
               pass
       if best_p>=0:
           apply best; cur=best_sc; best_score=min; _global_update; no_improve=0
       else:
           no_improve +=1
           # random trial
           p=rng.randint(N)
           apply; sc=_score(a)
           if sc < cur: accept (cur=sc; best update?) else with probability exp(-0.5*(sc-cur)/0.01?)
           if not accept revert
           if no_improve > 15: break
   return best_s
```
Flipping `a[p]^=1` works for float 0/1. But `a` is float; XOR with 1? In numpy, `a[p] ^= 1` may not in-place? Use `a[p] = 1.0 - a[p]`.
Same for s int: `s[p] = 1 - s[p]`.
After scan, if best_p found, need apply once. We must ensure s at scan end original; yes.
Applying:
```
s[p]=1-s[p]; a[p]=1-a[p]; a[n-1-p]=1-a[n-1-p]
```
`n-1-p` within.
Scanning all p with full score each pass; for N=511 and 10 passes 5k scores. Good.

But choosing best_sc initialized to cur. We only accept if <cur. If multiple flips, we revert each. Good.

Temperature for random moves: Score differences maybe 1e-4. Use delta = sc-cur. Accept if delta <0 or rng.random() < np.exp(-delta*50) and no_improve small. But could wander. Limit random.

Now, code details for stable sigmoid:
```
def _sigmoid_arr(z):
    z=np.clip(z, -50, 50)
    out=np.empty_like(z)
    pos = z >= 0
    out[pos] = 1.0/(1.0+np.exp(-z[pos]))
    ez=np.exp(z[~pos])
    out[~pos]=ez/(1.0+ez)
    return out
```

def _simple_pm_seq(length, seed):
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    x=rng.choice(np.array([-1,1], dtype=int), size=length)
    # optionally balance: if sum(x)>0 flip last etc.
    if x.sum()>0: idx=rng.randint(length); x[idx]=-1
    return x
```
Use for N=511/512. For mseq:
```
def _mseq_seq(m=9):
    n=(1<<m)-1
    state=(1<<m)-1
    taps=[m-1, m-5]
    x=np.empty(n,int)
    for i in range(n):
       x[i]=1 if (state&1) else -1
       fb = ((state>>taps[0]) ^ (state>>taps[1])) & 1
       state = (state >>1) | (fb << (m-1))
    return x
```
But if sequence repeats before n? Could check period; if not, still. For m=9, if primitive, period 511. We can also compute autocorrelation? No.

Now, implement `_z_from_antipal_sequence(a,N)`:
```
def _z_from_antipal_sequence(a,N):
    a=np.asarray(a,float); a=np.clip(a,0,None)
    if a.size != 2*N: ...
    C=1.0/N
    left=a[:N]
    right=a[N:2*N][::-1]
    # if pair sums differ, scale left to [0,C]: u=np.clip(left, eps, C-eps)
    eps=1e-15
    u=np.clip(left, eps, C-eps)
    z=np.log(u/(C-u))
    z=np.clip(z, -15,15)
    return z
```
If a is binary counts not normalized, pair sum N? For binary 0/1 counts, C for normalized is 1/N but left counts 0/1; clipping to C ~0.002 bad. So for binary counts, use `_z_from_s` not this. If using continuous normalized, C=1/N. In global best from smooth normalized. Good.

def _upsample(a, n):
    if a.size==n: return a
    x_old=np.linspace(0,1,len(a),endpoint=True); x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n,endpoint=True)
    return np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
```

Let's detail each function code.

`_next_pow2(m)`:
```
def _next_pow2(m):
    n=1
    while n < m:
        n <<= 1
    return n
```

`_conv_fft(a)`:
```
def _conv_fft(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    N=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)
    return c[:2*n-1]
```
Potential small negative due to FFT; max positive. Could `np.maximum(c,0)`? Convolution of nonnegative should be nonnegative but small negative -1e-16. max likely positive. For gradients, negative could affect. We can do `if c.size: c=np.where(c>0,c,0.0)` but that may bias. Small negative okay. In weights, c-mc negative. We'll clip c to >=0 in score? In `_score`, mx=max(c); if small negative not issue. For weights, c can tiny negative; use `cc=np.maximum(c,0.0)` when weights. Good.

`_score(a)`:
```
def _score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size==0: return np.inf
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)): a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1e12)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s<1e-12: return np.inf
    c=_conv_fft(a)
    if c.size==0: return np.inf
    m=float(np.max(c))
    if m<0: m=0.0
    return float(2.0*a.size*m/(s*s))
```
Clipping upper 1e12; for final 1000. For internal binary okay. If huge, s large; but overflow? If a up to 1e12, c up to 1e24, maybe overflow float? 1e24 <1e308, okay. But we usually normalized.

`_normalize(a)`:
```
def _normalize(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float).copy()
   if a.size==0: return a
   a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0); a=np.clip(a,0,1e6)
   s=a.sum()
   if s<1e-12: return np.ones(a.size)/a.size
   return a/s
```
For upsample, values positive.

`_global_update(a, score=None)`:
```
def _global_update(a, score=None):
    global _GLOBAL_BEST_A, _GLOBAL_BEST_SCORE, _GLOBAL_BEST_N
    if a is None: return
    a=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size==0: return
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s<0.01: return
    if score is None or not np.isfinite(score):
        score=_score(a)
    if np.isfinite(score) and score < _GLOBAL_BEST_SCORE:
        _GLOBAL_BEST_SCORE=float(score)
        _GLOBAL_BEST_A=a.copy()
        _GLOBAL_BEST_N=int(a.size)
```
Clipping to 1000 may lower values if a normalized huge? no. If a binary counts 1, no. If a from smooth somehow >1000? no.

But if a list from final with sum 1, clipping no effect.

`_grad_from_weights(w,a)`:
```
def _grad_from_weights(w, a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    w=np.asarray(w,float)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.array([],float)
    L=2*n-1
    if w.size < L:
       tmp=np.zeros(L); tmp[:w.size]=w; w=tmp
    else:
       w=w[:L]
    N=_next_pow2(2*n)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    fw=np.fft.rfft(w,N)
    x=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(fa)*fw,N)
    return 2.0*x[:n]
```
Need if N < w.size? N=next_pow2(2n) >=2n >2n-1, so okay.

`_weights_topsoft(c,T,K)`:
```
def _weights_topsoft(c,T,K):
    c=np.asarray(c,float); L=c.size
    if L==0: return np.array([],float)
    c=np.where(np.isfinite(c),c,0.0); np.maximum(c,0,c)
    if K is None or K>=L:
       idx=np.arange(L)
    else:
       K=max(1,int(K))
       if K==1:
          idx=np.array([int(np.argmax(c))])
       else:
          idx=np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
    vals=c[idx]
    vmax=float(vals.max())
    vmin=float(vals.min())
    scale=vmax-vmin
    if scale < 1e-15 or T <= 1e-8:
       q=np.zeros(L); q[idx]=1.0/idx.size; return q
    e=np.exp(float(T)*(vals-vmax)/scale)
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite:
       mask=vals >= vmax-1e-12; ...
    q=np.zeros(L); q[idx]=e/s; return q
```
If K=1 and top peak unique, q one-hot. Good.

In `_unrest_value_grad`, if x is a list? It will be ndarray from scipy; convert `x=np.asarray(x,float)`. Ensure length n-1. If not finite, set zeros.

```
def _unrest_value_grad(x):
    n=_UNREST_N
    T=_UNREST_T
    y=np.empty(n,float)
    y[0]=0.0
    if x.size:
       y[1:] = np.asarray(x,float).reshape(-1)
    y=np.where(np.isfinite(y),y,0.0)
    mxy=float(np.max(y))
    a=np.exp(y-mxy)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s<=0 or not finite:
       a=np.ones(n)/n
    else: a/=s
    c=_conv_fft(a)
    cc=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    mc=float(cc.max())
    if mc < _GLOBAL_BEST_SCORE/(2.0*n): _global_update(a, 2.0*n*mc)
    shift=cc-mc
    e=np.exp(T*shift)
    Z=e.sum()
    if Z<=0 or not finite:
       idx=np.argmax(cc); w=np.zeros_like(cc); w[idx]=1.0; f=mc
    else:
       q=e/Z; w=q*cc; f=float(mc + np.log(Z)/T)
    g2=_grad_from_weights(w,a)
    M=float(np.dot(a,g2))
    g=a*(g2-M)
    g=np.where(np.isfinite(g),g,0.0)
    return f, g[1:]
```
If n=1? We won't run. If T maybe 0? We set >0. If T very small, logZ/T huge? T e.g. 10. Good.

`_unrest_fun`:
```
def _unrest_fun(x):
    global _UNREST_CACHE_VERSION, _UNREST_CACHE_G
    f,g=_unrest_value_grad(x)
    _UNREST_CACHE_G=g
    _UNREST_CACHE_VERSION = _UNREST_CACHE_VERSION + 1
    return float(f)
```
`_unrest_jac`:
```
def _unrest_jac(x):
    global _UNREST_JAC_VERSION, _UNREST_CACHE_VERSION, _UNREST_CACHE_G
    if _UNREST_JAC_VERSION != _UNREST_CACHE_VERSION or _UNREST_CACHE_G is None:
        f,g=_unrest_value_grad(x)
        _UNREST_CACHE_G=g
        _UNREST_CACHE_VERSION += 1
    _UNREST_JAC_VERSION = _UNREST_CACHE_VERSION
    return _UNREST_CACHE_G
```
If fun called and then jac, versions match. If jac called without fun, recompute. If fun called again (version changed), jac recomputes. Good.

`_run_lbgfs_unrestricted(a,T,maxiter,deadline)`:
```
def _run_lbgfs_unrestricted(a,T,maxiter,deadline):
    global _UNREST_N, _UNREST_T, _OPT_DEADLINE, _UNREST_CACHE_*, _UNREST_JAC_*
    if _scipy_optimize is None: return
    if deadline is None: deadline=time.monotonic()+1.0
    if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 5: return
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    if a.size<2: return
    # normalize for start and y relative
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0); a=np.clip(a,0,None); s=a.sum(); if s<... a=uniform
    n=a.size
    _UNREST_N=n; _UNREST_T=float(T)
    _UNREST_CACHE_VERSION=0; _UNREST_JAC_VERSION=-1; _UNREST_CACHE_G=None
    eps=1e-300
    base=np.maximum(a, eps)
    y=np.log(base)
    y -= y[0]
    x0=np.ascontiguousarray(y[1:].copy())
    _OPT_DEADLINE=float(deadline)
    print start
    try:
      options={'maxiter': int(maxiter), 'maxfun': int(maxiter*20), 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-8, 'maxls':50}
      res=_scipy_optimize.minimize(_unrest_fun,x0,jac=_unrest_jac,method='L-BFGS-B',callback=_time_deadline_callback,options=options)
      y=np.empty(n); y[0]=0; y[1:]=np.asarray(res.x,float)
      a2=_y_to_a(y) # helper or inline
      sc=_score(a2); _global_update(a2,sc)
    except _StopOptimization:
      pass
    except Exception as e:
      print('unrest lbfgs error', e)
    # print global best
```

def _sine_anti(n, amp, phase=0.5):
 i=np.arange(n,float)
 theta=2*pi*(i+phase)/n
 a=(1.0+amp*np.sin(theta))/float(n)
 a=np.clip(a,0,None)
 s=a.sum()
 if s>0: a/=s
 else uniform
 return a

`_mseq_pm(m=9)`: implement. If m=9, n=511. Could use state. Let's choose taps [m-1, m-4] for x^9 + x^4 +1. If m not 9, general maybe x^m+x^{m-4}+1. For m=9 ok. Recurrence feedback = bit at top xor bit at m-4? Code:
```
def _mseq_pm(m=9):
    n=(1<<m)-1
    state=(1<<m)-1
    x=np.empty(n, dtype=np.int8)
    tap1=m-1
    tap2=m-4
    for i in range(n):
       x[i]=1 if (state & 1) else -1
       fb = ((state >> tap1) ^ (state >> tap2)) & 1
       state = (state >> 1) | (fb << tap1)
    return x
```
If not primitive, still. Could also if period != n: Use random.
Check period maybe after generating: If all zero? No. We can test x[:n//?]. Not.

Now, possible flaw: In smooth L-BFGS objective, `f = mc + logZ/T`. For T large, if c values ~1/n, differences small. We update global best with exact mc. However L-BFGS may not converge to exact max because f_T includes logZ/T, which for T large approximates max c + (log k_active)/T. As T finite, it may prefer multiple active peaks. Good for final. We can run increasing T.

In mirror, weights use topK and T. The objective being optimized is weighted average of c, not exact max. But line search evaluates exact max. Good.

Now, let's consider if gradient `_grad_from_weights` in smooth functions with `w=q*c` is correct for smooth f_T. If q=e/Z, f_T=mc+logZ/T? Actually Z=∑exp(T(cc-mc))=∑e. f_T=mc + (1/T)log Z. Derivative wrt a:
∂f/∂a = (1/T)∂logZ/∂a = (1/T)∑ q_t T ∂cc_t/∂a = ∑ q ∂c_t/∂a = 2∑q c? Wait ∂c_t/∂a = 2D_t a (not multiplied by c_t). For smooth over c_t directly: q_t=exp(T c_t)/Z. ∂f/∂a = 2∑ q_t D_t a. Not 2∑ q_t c_t D_t a. I made a mistake! Let's re-derive.
Objective f_T(a) = (1/T) log ∑_t exp(T c_t(a)). q_t = exp(T c_t)/Z. Gradient =∑ q_t ∂c_t/∂a = 2∑q_t D_t a. Correct. Not weighted by c.
Earlier I confused with maximizing log product? For softmax probabilities over peaks with normalized range, weights q over c; gradient wrt a is 2 D_a(q) (not q c).
So in smooth functions, F_a = _grad_from_weights(q, a) if `_grad` returns 2∑wD with w=q. Not w=q*c. We used w=q*c incorrectly.
What about y-smooth using log-sum-exp of c? Yes F_a=2∑q D_t a.
For smooth approximation of max, active all equal m, w=q; constant m doesn't matter. So no multiply by c.

Then gradient in y for softmax a: F_a=2W. M=∑a F = 2∑q c. (since ∑a D_t a = c_t). g_y = a(F_a - M) = a(2W - 2∑q c). Good. In code `_grad_from_weights(q,a)` returns 2W. M should be dot(a,F) = 2∑q c. Good. So w should be q, not q*c.

For mirror descent, weights should be q (softmax over c), not q*c? To approximate gradient of f_T, use q. We used q in mirror w=q*c; wrong. Should be q. But if q computed over top peaks normalized c range, high peaks dominate via q. No extra c scaling. Let's correct.

In `_unrest_value_grad`:
- q=e/Z; w=q; g2=_grad_from_weights(q,a); M=dot(a,g2). Good.

For mirror: F=_grad_from_weights(q,a). Good.

Check derivative of f_T with stable shift: q independent of shift. Good.

Now, in smooth f_T, if T moderate, should we use c directly or normalized? q=exp(T(cc-mc)) with c absolute. If c values around 0.001 and differences maybe 1e-5, T=100 gives q slightly. In runs, set T_abs = t_desired * n (or * n/(1?)). For T_desired 5-500. Let's set in smooth functions `T = _UNREST_T * n`? Or set runs directly with T_abs. To avoid confusion, we can normalize c inside smooth functions: compute `c_scaled = c / max(c)`? q=exp(Tabs*(cc - mc)/scale). That makes T_desired dimensionless. But objective f should approximate max c; if we use scaled weights, gradient? The smooth objective would be logsumexp(T (c - mc)/scale) not exact f_T; gradient = (1/scale) 2∑q D a. The constant 1/scale doesn't affect L-BFGS direction? It does affect step size; L-BFGS adapts. We could just use weights q = exp(T_desired*(cc-mc)/scale), and objective value for optimization could be scaled; but to track exact best, value not critical. For gradient direction, constant factor okay. L-BFGS can handle scale. To make f value meaningful, set f = mc + logZ/T_desired? Not consistent if divide by scale. Simpler: use q = exp(T_desired*(cc-mc)/scale) for weights, and for f return hc + logZ/T_desider (or scaled?), but gradient computed with q only; L-BFGS uses f value and gradient; if f value not derivative of q? L-BFGS assumes gradient matches objective. Need consistency.

Implement local search:
```
def _antips_binary_search(s, deadline, time_limit, seed=1):
  global _OPT? no
  s=s.copy().astype(np.int8)
  N=s.size; n=2*N
  a=_anti_sequence_raw(s)
  cur=_score(a)
  best_score=cur; best_s=s.copy()
  _global_update(a,cur)
  t0=time.monotonic(); t_end=min(deadline, t0+time_limit)
  rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
  stall=0
  while time.monotonic() < t_end:
    order=rng.permutation(N)
    best_p=-1; best_sc=cur
    for p in order:
       # flip
       a[p]=1.0-a[p]; a[n-1-p]=1.0-a[n-1-p]; s[p]=1-s[p]
       sc=_score(a)
       a[p]=1.0-a[p]; a[n-1-p]=1.0-a[n-1-p]; s[p]=1-s[p]
       if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
          best_sc=sc; best_p=p
       if time.monotonic() >= t_end: break
    if best_p >=0:
       p=best_p; apply; cur=best_sc; best_s=s.copy(); best_score=cur; stall=0; _global_update(a,cur)
    else:
       stall +=1
       # random move with acceptance
       p=int(rng.randint(N))
       apply; sc=_score(a)
       if sc <= cur or (stall<20 and rng.random() < math.exp(-50*max(0,sc-cur))):
           cur=sc; best_s=s.copy(); best_score=min(best_score,cur); _global_update(a,cur); stall=0
       else:
           revert
       if stall > 25: break
  # update best_s final? a is current; score maybe cur; if cur<best_score? update
  _global_update(a, cur)
  return best_s
```
If random accepted worse, best_s remains old but current a worse; subsequent scans from worse. To keep best, after accepting worse, if stop? Could revert to best if many. Simpler: only accept random if sc<=cur, no worse. But may escape. We can accept but keep current; if stall long, reset to best_s. Use:
```
if accept: cur=sc ... else revert
if stall>10: reset a,_s=best_s.copy, _anti_sequence_raw, cur=best_score
```
Okay.

Applying flip helper inline. Use float.

```
def _balanced_pm(length, seed):
   rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
   half=(length-1)//2
   x=np.ones(length,dtype=np.int8)
   # choose half to be -1
   idx=rng.choice(length, half, replace=False)
   x[idx]=-1
   rng.shuffle(x)
   return x
```
This ensures balanced low DC, maybe good. Use for random starts.

def _to_list(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    if a.size==0: return [0.0]
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0.0)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<0.01:
       # scale up? evaluate inf if sum<0.01. Our normalized sum1. But if all zero, return uniform?
       a=np.ones(a.size)/max(a.size,1)
    return [float(v) for v in a]
```

Now, let's inspect derivative in anti-pal logit with smooth f. If f is logsumexp scale fixed, gradient wrt a F=2∑qD. But z changes b with sum constant. `grad=(uv/s)*(Fleft-Fright)`. Should there be a factor? Let's verify with simple: b_left=u, b_right=v. ∂b_left/∂z=uv, ∂b_right/∂z=-uv. a=b/s, s=1 (exact); ∂a_left=uv/s. Derivative f=∑ F_a_i ∂a_i = F_left uv/s + F_right(-uv/s). Correct. If s not 1, because a=b/s and f homogeneous, derivative of f with respect to b scaled by 1/s^2; using F_a at a (derivative wrt a) times da/db = 1/s DB (since sum db=0) gives correct. Good.

In unrestricted y-smooth, derivation: a=e_y/s. ∂a_i/s? If y changes, F_a=2∑qD at a. Derivative = F_a_i a_i - a_i M (M=∑a F). Yes. Code `g=a*(g2-M)`. Good.

If top range maybe 1e-7 and max 1e-3, 1e-4*max=1e-7; okay. Use 1e-5*max? Let's use `scale = max(scale, 1e-6 * mx, 1e-12)`. That avoids too tiny. T max 1000 => T/scale*logit up to 1000* c_range/scale; if range ~scale, 1000, underflow okay. Good.

```python
"""
Search for low-peak nonnegative step functions: combined softmax L-BFGS,
adaptive mirror descent, antipalindromic binary local search, and
continuous antipalindromic logit optimization.
"""

import time
import math

import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy import optimize as _SCIPY_OPT
except Exception:
    _SCIPY_OPT = None


class _StopOptimization(Exception):
    pass


# Global incumbent
_GLOBAL_BEST_A = None
_GLOBAL_BEST_SCORE = np.inf
_GLOBAL_BEST_N = 0

# Deadline for scipy callbacks
_OPT_DEADLINE = 0.0

# Unrestricted softmax/L-BFGS state
_UNREST_N = 0
_UNREST_T = 1.0
_UNREST_SCALE = 1.0
_UNREST_CACHE_G = None
_UNREST_CACHE_VERSION = 0
_UNREST_JAC_VERSION = -1

# Antipalindromic softmax/L-BFGS state
_ANT_N = 0
_ANT_T = 1.0
_ANT_SCALE = 1.0
_ANT_CACHE_G = None
_ANT_CACHE_VERSION = 0
_ANT_JAC_VERSION = -1


def _next_pow2(m):
    n = 1
    while n < m:
        n <<= 1
    return n


def _conv_fft(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    N = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)
    return c[:2 * n - 1]


def _score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1.0e12)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1.0e-12:
        return np.inf
    c = _conv_fft(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if m < 0.0:
        m = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * a.size * m / (s * s))


def _normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1.0e6)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1.0e-12:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(max(a.size, 1))
    return a / s


def _global_update(a, score=None):
    global _GLOBAL_BEST_A, _GLOBAL_BEST_SCORE, _GLOBAL_BEST_N
    if a is None:
        return
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if a.sum() <= 0.01:
        return
    if score is None or not np.isfinite(score):
        score = _score(a)
    if np.isfinite(score) and score < _GLOBAL_BEST_SCORE:
        _GLOBAL_BEST_A = a.copy()
        _GLOBAL_BEST_SCORE = float(score)
        _GLOBAL_BEST_N = int(a.size)


def _grad_from_weights(w, a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if w.size < L:
        tmp = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        tmp[:w.size] = w
        w = tmp
    else:
        w = w[:L].copy()
    N = _next_pow2(2 * n)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    x = np.fft.irfft(np.conj(fa) * fw, N)
    return 2.0 * x[:n]


def _active_range(c, K=32):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    if c.size == 0:
        return 1.0e-12
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    mx = float(c.max())
    K = max(1, min(int(K), c.size))
    if K == c.size:
        vals = c
    else:
        vals = c[np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]]
    sc = float(vals.max() - vals.min())
    if not np.isfinite(sc) or sc <= 0.0:
        sc = 1.0e-6 * max(mx, 1.0e-12)
    sc = max(sc, 1.0e-6 * max(mx, 1.0e-12), 1.0e-12)
    return sc


def _make_smoothing_scale(a):
    c = _conv_fft(a)
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return _active_range(c, 32)


def _weights_topsoft(c, T, K):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    L = c.size
    if L == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    K = int(K)
    if K <= 1:
        idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))], dtype=np.int64)
        vals = c[idx]
        q = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        q[idx[0]] = 1.0
        return q
    if K >= L:
        idx = np.arange(L)
    else:
        idx = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
    vals = c[idx]
    vmax = float(vals.max())
    vmin = float(vals.min())
    sc = vmax - vmin
    if sc <= 1.0e-15 * max(vmax, 1.0e-12) or T <= 1.0e-8:
        q = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        q[idx] = 1.0 / float(idx.size)
        return q
    e = np.exp(float(T) * (vals - vmax) / sc)
    z = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(z) or z <= 0.0:
        q = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        mask = vals >= vmax - 1.0e-12
        if not np.any(mask):
            q[idx[int(np.argmax(vals))]] = 1.0
        else:
            q[idx[mask]] = 1.0 / float(np.count_nonzero(mask))
        return q
    q = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    q[idx] = e / z
    return q


def _sigmoid_arr(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0)
    out = np.empty_like(z)
    pos = z >= 0.0
    out[pos] = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-z[pos]))
    ez = np.exp(z[~pos])
    out[~pos] = ez / (1.0 + ez)
    return out


def _antips_from_z(z, N):
    N = int(N)
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if z.size < N:
        z = np.pad(z, (0, N - z.size), mode="constant")
    else:
        z = z[:N]
    C = 1.0 / float(N) if N > 0 else 1.0
    sig = _sigmoid_arr(z)
    u = C * sig
    v = C - u
    a = np.empty(2 * N, dtype=np.float64)
    a[:N] = u
    a[N:] = v[::-1]
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        a.fill(1.0 / float(max(2 * N, 1)))
    else:
        a = a / s
    return a


def _z_from_antipal_sequence(a, N):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    if N <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size != 2 * N:
        # threshold by local comparison on first 2N entries if possible
        a = a[:2 * N]
        if a.size < 2 * N:
            pad = np.zeros(2 * N - a.size, dtype=np.float64)
            a = np.concatenate((a, pad))
    C = 1.0 / float(N)
    eps = 1.0e-12 * C
    left = a[:N]
    left = np.clip(left, eps, C - eps)
    z = np.log(left / (C - left))
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
    return np.clip(z, -15.0, 15.0)


def _to_list(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return [0.0]
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        a = np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(max(a.size, 1))
    return [float(v) for v in a]


def _y_to_a(y):
    n = int(y.size)
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64)
    y = np.where(np.isfinite(y), y, 0.0)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    y = y - float(np.max(y))
    a = np.exp(y)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    else:
        a = a / s
    return a


def _a_to_x(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    eps = 1.0e-300
    base = np.maximum(a, eps)
    y = np.log(base)
    y -= y[0]
    y = np.where(np.isfinite(y), y, 0.0)
    y[0] = 0.0
    return y[1:].copy()


def _time_deadline_callback(xk):
    if time.monotonic() >= _OPT_DEADLINE:
        raise _StopOptimization


def _unrest_value_grad(x):
    n = int(_UNREST_N)
    T = float(_UNREST_T)
    scale = float(_UNREST_SCALE)
    if n <= 1:
        return 0.0, np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if T <= 1.0e-12:
        T = 1.0e-12
    if scale <= 1.0e-300:
        scale = 1.0e-12

    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if x.size < n - 1:
        x = np.pad(x, (0, n - 1 - x.size), mode="constant")
    else:
        x = x[:n - 1]

    y = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    y[0] = 0.0
    y[1:] = x
    y = np.where(np.isfinite(y), y, 0.0)
    y = y - float(np.max(y))
    a = np.exp(y)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    else:
        a = a / s

    c = _conv_fft(a)
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    mc = float(c.max())
    if mc < _GLOBAL_BEST_SCORE / (2.0 * float(n)):
        _global_update(a, 2.0 * float(n) * mc)

    zt = (T / scale) * (c - mc)
    e = np.exp(zt)
    Z = float(e.sum())
    if Z <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(Z):
        idx = int(np.argmax(c))
        w = np.zeros_like(c)
        w[idx] = 1.0
        f = float(mc)
    else:
        w = e / Z
        f = float(mc + (scale / T) * math.log(Z))

    F = _grad_from_weights(w, a)
    M = float(np.dot(a, F))
    g = a * (F - M)
    g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
    return f, g[1:]


def _unrest_fun(x):
    global _UNREST_CACHE_VERSION, _UNREST_CACHE_G
    f, g = _unrest_value_grad(x)
    _UNREST_CACHE_G = g
    _UNREST_CACHE_VERSION = _UNREST_CACHE_VERSION + 1
    return float(f)


def _unrest_jac(x):
    global _UNREST_JAC_VERSION, _UNREST_CACHE_VERSION, _UNREST_CACHE_G
    if _UNREST_JAC_VERSION != _UNREST_CACHE_VERSION or _UNREST_CACHE_G is None:
        f, g = _unrest_value_grad(x)
        _UNREST_CACHE_G = g
        _UNREST_CACHE_VERSION = _UNREST_CACHE_VERSION + 1
    _UNREST_JAC_VERSION = _UNREST_CACHE_VERSION
    return _UNREST_CACHE_G


def _run_lbgfs_unrestricted(a, T, maxiter, deadline):
    global _UNREST_N, _UNREST_T, _UNREST_SCALE
    global _OPT_DEADLINE
    global _UNREST_CACHE_G, _UNREST_CACHE_VERSION, _UNREST_JAC_VERSION

    if _SCIPY_OPT is None:
        return
    if deadline is None:
        deadline = time.monotonic() + 1.0
    if time.monotonic() >= max(deadline - 5.0, time.monotonic()):
        return

    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size < 2:
        return
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    # tiny floor makes L-BFGS work better from nearly sparse seeds
    a = a * (1.0 - 1.0e-8) + 1.0e-8 / float(a.size)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        a = np.ones(a.size) / a.size
    else:
        a = a / s

    n = a.size
    _UNREST_N = n
    _UNREST_T = float(T)
    _UNREST_SCALE = float(_make_smoothing_scale(a))
    _UNREST_CACHE_G = None
    _UNREST_CACHE_VERSION = 0
    _UNREST_JAC_VERSION = -1

    x0 = _a_to_x(a)
    _OPT_DEADLINE = float(deadline)
    print(f"[L-BFGS unrestricted] n={n}, T={T}, scale={_UNREST_SCALE:.3e}", flush=True)

    try:
        options = {
            "maxiter": int(maxiter),
            "maxfun": int(maxiter * 20),
            "ftol": 1.0e-10,
            "gtol": 1.0e-6,
            "maxls": 50,
        }
        res = _SCIPY_OPT.minimize(
            _unrest_fun,
            x0,
            jac=_unrest_jac,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            callback=_time_deadline_callback,
            options=options,
        )
        y = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
        y[0] = 0.0
        y[1:] = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        a2 = _y_to_a(y)
        sc = _score(a2)
        _global_update(a2, sc)
    except _StopOptimization:
        pass
    except Exception as exc:
        print("[L-BFGS unrestricted] exception:", repr(exc), flush=True)

    print(f"[L-BFGS unrestricted] done, best={_GLOBAL_BEST_SCORE:.10f}", flush=True)


def _antips_value_grad(z):
    N = int(_ANT_N)
    n = 2 * N
    T = float(_ANT_T)
    scale = float(_ANT_SCALE)
    if N <= 0:
        return 0.0, np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if T <= 1.0e-12:
        T = 1.0e-12
    if scale <= 1.0e-300:
        scale = 1.0e-12

    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if z.size < N:
        z = np.pad(z, (0, N - z.size), mode="constant")
    else:
        z = z[:N]
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0)

    C = 1.0 / float(N)
    sig = _sigmoid_arr(z)
    u = C * sig
    v = C - u
    b = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    b[:N] = u
    b[N:] = v[::-1]
    s = float(b.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        s = 1.0
    else:
        a = b / s

    c = _conv_fft(a)
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    mc = float(c.max())
    if mc < _GLOBAL_BEST_SCORE / (2.0 * float(n)):
        _global_update(a, 2.0 * float(n) * mc)

    zt = (T / scale) * (c - mc)
    e = np.exp(zt)
    Z = float(e.sum())
    if Z <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(Z):
        idx = int(np.argmax(c))
        w = np.zeros_like(c)
        w[idx] = 1.0
        f = float(mc)
    else:
        w = e / Z
        f = float(mc + (scale / T) * math.log(Z))

    F = _grad_from_weights(w, a)
    rightF = F[N:n][::-1]
    uv = u * (C - u)
    grad = (uv / s) * (F[:N] - rightF)
    grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
    return f, grad


def _antips_fun(z):
    global _ANT_CACHE_VERSION, _ANT_CACHE_G
    f, g = _antips_value_grad(z)
    _ANT_CACHE_G = g
    _ANT_CACHE_VERSION = _ANT_CACHE_VERSION + 1
    return float(f)


def _antips_jac(z):
    global _ANT_JAC_VERSION, _ANT_CACHE_VERSION, _ANT_CACHE_G
    if _ANT_JAC_VERSION != _ANT_CACHE_VERSION or _ANT_CACHE_G is None:
        f, g = _antips_value_grad(z)
        _ANT_CACHE_G = g
        _ANT_CACHE_VERSION = _ANT_CACHE_VERSION + 1
    _ANT_JAC_VERSION = _ANT_CACHE_VERSION
    return _ANT_CACHE_G


def _run_lbgfs_antipal(N, z, T, maxiter, deadline):
    global _ANT_N, _ANT_T, _ANT_SCALE
    global _OPT_DEADLINE
    global _ANT_CACHE_G, _ANT_CACHE_VERSION, _ANT_JAC_VERSION

    if _SCIPY_OPT is None:
        return
    if deadline is None:
        deadline = time.monotonic() + 1.0
    if int(N) <= 8:
        return
    if time.monotonic() >= max(deadline - 5.0, time.monotonic()):
        return

    N = int(N)
    a = _antips_from_z(z, N)
    _ANT_N = N
    _ANT_T = float(T)
    _ANT_SCALE = float(_make_smoothing_scale(a))
    _ANT_CACHE_G = None
    _ANT_CACHE_VERSION = 0
    _ANT_JAC_VERSION = -1

    z0 = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if z0.size < N:
        z0 = np.pad(z0, (0, N - z0.size), mode="constant")
    else:
        z0 = z0[:N]
    z0 = np.clip(z0, -30.0, 30.0)

    _OPT_DEADLINE = float(deadline)
    print(f"[L-BFGS antipal] N={N}, n={2*N}, T={T}, scale={_ANT_SCALE:.3e}", flush=True)

    try:
        options = {
            "maxiter": int(maxiter),
            "maxfun": int(maxiter * 20),
            "ftol": 1.0e-10,
            "gtol": 1.0e-6,
            "maxls": 50,
        }
        res = _SCIPY_OPT.minimize(
            _antips_fun,
            z0,
            jac=_antips_jac,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            callback=_time_deadline_callback,
            options=options,
        )
        z1 = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        a1 = _antips_from_z(z1, N)
        sc = _score(a1)
        _global_update(a1, sc)
    except _StopOptimization:
        pass
    except Exception as exc:
        print("[L-BFGS antipal] exception:", repr(exc), flush=True)

    print(f"[L-BFGS antipal] done, best={_GLOBAL_BEST_SCORE:.10f}", flush=True)


def _optimize_mirror_unrest(a, deadline, T0=20.0, T1=300.0, K=16, step0=0.5):
    global _GLOBAL_BEST_A, _GLOBAL_BEST_SCORE, _GLOBAL_BEST_N

    a = _normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    if n < 2:
        return
    if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
        return

    cur = _score(a)
    _global_update(a, cur)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = cur
    step = float(step0)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(31415)

    it = 0
    last_improve = 0
    patience = 700

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        c = _conv_fft(a)
        c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
        np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
        mc = float(c.max())
        sc = 2.0 * float(n) * mc
        if sc < best_sc - 1.0e-12:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a.copy()
            last_improve = it
            _global_update(a, sc)

        T = float(T0) + (float(T1) - float(T0)) * min(1.0, it / 20000.0)
        q = _weights_topsoft(c, T, K)
        F = _grad_from_weights(q, a)
        z = F - float(np.mean(F))
        zmax = float(np.max(np.abs(z)))
        if not np.isfinite(zmax) or zmax <= 1.0e-14:
            if it - last_improve > patience:
                break
            continue
        z = z / zmax

        if np.min(a) < 1.0e-10:
            a = a + 1.0e-10
            a = a / a.sum()

        alphas = [0.5 * step, step, 2.0 * step]
        chosen_a = None
        chosen_sc = sc
        chosen_alpha = None
        for alpha in alphas:
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                break
            coeff = np.exp(-alpha * z)
            an = a * coeff
            an = np.where(np.isfinite(an), an, 0.0)
            s = float(an.sum())
            if s <= 1.0e-12:
                continue
            an = an / s
            asc = _score(an)
            if asc < chosen_sc - 1.0e-12:
                chosen_sc = asc
                chosen_a = an
                chosen_alpha = alpha

        if chosen_a is not None:
            a = chosen_a
            sc = chosen_sc
            _global_update(a, sc)
            last_improve = it
            if chosen_alpha == alphas[-1]:
                step = min(4.0, step * 1.4)
            elif chosen_alpha == alphas[0]:
                step = max(0.05, step * 0.7)
            else:
                step = min(4.0, step * 1.08)
        else:
            step = max(0.03, step * 0.8)
            if it - last_improve > patience:
                noise = rng.standard_normal(n)
                p = best_a * np.exp(0.04 * noise)
                p = np.clip(p, 0.0, None)
                ps = float(p.sum())
                if ps > 1.0e-12:
                    p = p / ps
                    a = p + 1.0e-10
                    a = a / a.sum()
                else:
                    a = best_a.copy()
                patience = int(patience * 1.25)
                step = max(0.1, min(1.5, step0))

    _global_update(best_a, best_sc)


def _sine_anti(n, amp, phase=0.5):
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    theta = 2.0 * math.pi * (i + float(phase)) / float(n)
    a = (1.0 + float(amp) * np.sin(theta)) / float(n)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s > 0.01:
        a = a / s
    else:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return a


def _upsample_to(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == n:
        return _normalize(a)
    a = _normalize(a)
    x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size, endpoint=True)
    x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=True)
    return _normalize(np.interp(x1, x0, a))


def _mseq_pm(m=9):
    n = (1 << m) - 1
    state = (1 << m) - 1
    x = np.empty(n, dtype=np.int8)
    tap1 = m - 1
    tap2 = m - 4
    for i in range(n):
        x[i] = 1 if (state & 1) else -1
        fb = ((state >> tap1) ^ (state >> tap2)) & 1
        state = (state >> 1) | (fb << tap1)
    # If the generation is bad, still return; local optimization can repair it.
    return x


def _balanced_pm(length, seed):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    x = np.ones(int(length), dtype=np.int8)
    half = max(0, (int(length) - 1) // 2)
    if half > 0:
        idx = rng.choice(int(length), half, replace=False)
        x[idx] = -1
    rng.shuffle(x)
    return x


def _s_from_pm(x):
    x = np.asarray(x).reshape(-1)
    return (x < 0).astype(np.int8)


def _anti_sequence_raw(s):
    s = np.asarray(s).reshape(-1).astype(np.float64)
    N = s.size
    a = np.empty(2 * N, dtype=np.float64)
    a[:N] = 1.0 - s
    a[N:] = s[::-1]
    return a


def _s_from_sequence(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size < 2:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.int8)
    N = a.size // 2
    left = a[:N]
    right = a[N:2 * N][::-1]
    return (left < right).astype(np.int8)


def _z_from_s(s, bound=8.0):
    s = np.asarray(s).reshape(-1)
    z = np.where(s == 0, float(bound), -float(bound)).astype(np.float64)
    return z


def _antips_binary_search(s, deadline, time_limit, seed=1):
    if int(time_limit) < 1:
        return np.asarray(s).copy()
    s = np.asarray(s).reshape(-1).astype(np.int8).copy()
    N = s.size
    if N < 2:
        return s
    n = 2 * N
    a = _anti_sequence_raw(s)
    cur = _score(a)
    _global_update(a, cur)
    best_s = s.copy()
    best_sc = cur

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    t_end = min(float(deadline), t0 + float(time_limit))
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    stall = 0

    while time.monotonic() < t_end:
        order = rng.permutation(N)
        best_p = -1
        best_sc_candidate = cur
        for p in order:
            p = int(p)
            a[p] = 1.0 - a[p]
            a[n - 1 - p] = 1.0 - a[n - 1 - p]
            s[p] = 1 - s[p]
            sc = _score(a)
            a[p] = 1.0 - a[p]
            a[n - 1 - p] = 1.0 - a[n - 1 - p]
            s[p] = 1 - s[p]
            if sc < best_sc_candidate - 1.0e-12:
                best_sc_candidate = sc
                best_p = p
            if time.monotonic() >= t_end:
                break

        if best_p >= 0:
            p = best_p
            a[p] = 1.0 - a[p]
            a[n - 1 - p] = 1.0 - a[n - 1 - p]
            s[p] = 1 - s[p]
            cur = best_sc_candidate
            if cur < best_sc - 1.0e-15:
                best_sc = cur
                best_s = s.copy()
            _global_update(a, cur)
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1
            p = int(rng.randint(N))
            a[p] = 1.0 - a[p]
            a[n - 1 - p] = 1.0 - a[n - 1 - p]
            s[p] = 1 - s[p]
            sc = _score(a)
            accept = False
            if sc <= cur + 1.0e-15:
                accept = True
            elif stall < 25:
                try:
                    accept = bool(rng.random() < math.exp(-30.0 * max(0.0, sc - cur)))
                except Exception:
                    accept = False
            if accept:
                cur = sc
                if sc < best_sc - 1.0e-15:
                    best_sc = sc
                    best_s = s.copy()
                _global_update(a, cur)
                stall = 0
            else:
                a[p] = 1.0 - a[p]
                a[n - 1 - p] = 1.0 - a[n - 1 - p]
                s[p] = 1 - s[p]

            if stall > 12:
                s = best_s.copy()
                a = _anti_sequence_raw(s)
                cur = best_sc
                stall = 0
            if stall > 40:
                break

    _global_update(a, cur)
    return best_s


def _scan_length(n, deadline, prev_a=None):
    best = None
    best_sc = np.inf
    n = int(n)
    if n < 2:
        return None

    def add_candidate(arr):
        nonlocal best, best_sc
        arr = _normalize(arr)
        sc = _score(arr)
        _global_update(arr, sc)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best = arr.copy()

    if prev_a is not None:
        arr = prev_a if np.asarray(prev_a).size == n else _upsample_to(prev_a, n)
        add_candidate(arr)

    amps = [0.4, 0.6, 0.7, 0.75, 0.8, 0.85, 0.9, 0.95, 1.0, 1.2, 1.5]
    phases = [0.0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75]
    for amp in amps:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        for ph in phases:
            add_candidate(_sine_anti(n, amp, ph))

    rng = np.random.RandomState(1000 + n)
    for _ in range(4):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.55, 1.25))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0))
        add_candidate(_sine_anti(n, amp, ph))

    if n % 2 == 0:
        N = n // 2
        if N >= 2:
            # A few random binary antipalindromic seeds.
            for _ in range(4):
                if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                    break
                s = rng.randint(0, 2, size=N).astype(np.int8)
                add_candidate(_anti_sequence_raw(s))

    return best
```
